世足賽訂單維繫營運，維持 2Q26 稅後 EPS 為 3.21 元之預估，
年增 28.4% 
聚陽 5 月營收 27.09 億元，月增 3.9%，年增 8.8%，營收達成率 65%，符
合預期，世足賽訂單貢獻維繫營運，維持 2Q26 淡季營收 81.77 億元之預估，
季減 8.1%，年增 6.3%，毛利率維持 23.8%，營業利益 10.77 億元，季減
21.8%，年增 14.4%，稅後 EPS 維持 3.21 元，季減 26.3%，年增 28.4%。 
預估 3Q26 稅後 EPS 下修 5.6%至 4.09 元，但年增 17.6% 
3Q26 傳統旺季，客戶庫存偏低，回補庫存訂單需求維繫營運，惟美伊和談
進度干擾，品牌傾向短單急單的下單模式，預估出貨量由年增高個位數下修
至年增中個位數，預估 3Q26 營收下修 3%至 93.63 億元，季增 14.5%，年
增 5.3%，毛利率下修 0.9ppt 至 24.7%，營業利益下修 5.5%至 13.51 億元，
季增 25.4%，年增 23.7%，稅後 EPS 下修 5.6%至 4.09 元，季增 27.6%，
年增 17.6%。3Q26 營收及獲利仍維持年增格局，營運尚屬穩健。 
美伊和談變數干擾造成波折，但復甦方向不變，評價偏低 
展望 2026 年，聚陽的品牌客戶庫存陸續去化，回補庫存的訂單需求仍在，
日系客戶訂單穩健，可成長雙位數，來自 Dick’s Sport 訂單亦可年增雙位數，
Under Armour 訂單有機會年增 50%，Gap 旗下運動服飾品牌 Athleta 訂單
恢復增長，維繫營運基本盤，美伊和談變數造成波折，但復甦方向不變，預
估 2026 年營收 346.3 億元，年增 0.6%，稅後 EPS 為 15.45 元，年增 5.3%，
重啟成長，目前本益比 14.6 倍，在 14 至 24 倍歷史本益比區間下緣，評價
偏低，投資價值浮現，重申買進建議，目標價維持 310 元(20 倍 PER X 
2026 年稅後 EPS)。

元大觀點 
⧫  
毛利率優於預期且業外利益貢獻，1Q26 稅後 EPS 為 4.35 元，季增
23.5%，年減 15.5%，優於元大預期 8.8%。世足賽訂單維繫營運，維
持 2Q26 稅後 EPS 為 3.21 元之預估，年增 28.4%。 
⧫  
預估 3Q26 稅後 EPS 反映 ASP 年減下修 5.6%至 4.09 元，年增
17.6%，估 2026 年稅後 EPS 為 15.45 元，年增 5.5%，恢復正成長。 
⧫  
美伊和談變數干擾造成波折，但復甦方向不變，本益比 14.6 倍，評價
偏低，目標價維持 310 元，基於 20 倍本益比、2026 年 EPS 評價。

營運分析  
毛利率優於預期且業外利益貢獻，1Q26 稅後 EPS 為 4.35 元，季增 23.5%，年減
15.5%，優於預期 8.8% 
聚陽 1Q26 營收 88.97 億元(原估 90.36 億元)，季增 12.3%，年減 10.4%，低於預期 1.5%，毛利率
僅季減 0.6ppt 至 26.1%(原估 25.3%)，年減 1.7ppt，優於預期，而費用控管好轉，營益率季增
2ppt 至 15.5%(原估 14.7%)，年減 0.4ppt，營業利益 13.77 億元(原估 13.27 億元)，季增 29.1%，
年減 12.8%，優於預期 3.8%，業外匯兌利益貢獻約 2800 萬元，稅後 EPS 為 4.35 元(原估 4.0 元)，
季增 23.5%，年減 15.5%，優於元大預期 8.8%。生產效率提升及產品組合較佳，帶動毛利率優於
預期。

世足賽訂單維繫營運，彌補戰爭變數干擾，維持 2Q26 稅後 EPS 為 3.21 元之預估，
年增 28.4% 
聚陽公布 2026 年 5 月營收 27.09 億元，月增 3.9%，年增 8.8%，以原預估 2Q26 營收 81.77 億元
(季減 8.1%，年增 6.3%)計算，營收達成率 65%，符合預期，營收月增，乃 4 月為季節交替傳統淡
季，5 月出貨逐漸轉旺。其中 5 月出貨量年增 6%多，匯率有利約 2%多，ASP 則年持平。來自世
足賽訂單貢獻維繫營運，彌補美伊戰爭變數干擾，維持 2Q26 淡季營收 81.77 億元之預估，季減
8.1%，年增 6.3%，毛利率維持 23.8%，營益率維持 13.2%，營業利益維持 10.77 億元，季減
21.8%，年增 14.4%，稅後 EPS 維持 3.21 元，季減 26.3%，年增 28.4%。2Q26 營收及獲利維持
年增格局，營運穩健。

美伊和談變數干擾，預估 3Q26 稅後 EPS 下修 5.6%至 4.09 元，但年增 17.6% 
展望 3Q26 傳統旺季，客戶庫存偏低，預期回補庫存訂單需求維繫營運，惟最大變數仍係美伊 和談
進度干擾，油價維持高檔，引發通膨升溫排擠消費之擔憂，造成市場疑慮，品牌下單略顯猶豫，傾
向短單急單的下單模式，能見度不高，來自品牌客戶整體出貨規劃作調整幅度約 3%，預估出貨量
由年增高個位數下修至年增中個位數，而台幣匯率較 2025 年同期回貶，彌補 ASP 年減 3%之拖累，
預估 3Q26 營收下修 3%至 93.63 億元(原估 96.49 億元)，季增 14.5%，年增 5.3%，毛利率下修
0.9ppt 至 24.7%(原估 25.6%)，營益率下修 0.4ppt 至 14.4%(原估 14.8%)，營業利益下修 5.5%至
13.51 億元(原估 14.3 億元)，季增 25.4%，年增 23.7%，稅後 EPS 下修 5.6%至 4.09 元(原估 4.33
元)，季增 27.6%，年增 17.6%。3Q26 毛利率下修，係反映 ASP 年減，削弱部分獲利動能，不過
若客戶有急單挹注，聚陽仍有產能可因應，進而有營收及毛利率上修機會。 3Q26 營收雖下修，但
營收及獲利仍維持年增格局，營運尚屬穩健。

### Page 4

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：成衣 2026 年 6 月 3 日 Makalot 第4 頁，共 17頁
預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 上修 0.4%至 15.45 元，年增 5.3%
展望 2026 年，美伊和談進度不確定性恐影響 2H26 美國消費動能以及聚陽來自美系客戶的訂單展
望，不過品牌客戶庫存陸續去化，回補庫存的訂單需求仍在，只是尚待觀察戰爭發展，壓抑短期下
單意願，惟聚陽日系客戶訂單穩健，可成長雙位數，來自 Dick’s Sport 訂單亦可年增雙位數，
Under Armour 訂單有機會年增 50%，Gap 旗下運動服飾品牌 Athleta 訂單恢復增長，運動服飾訂
單需求維繫營運基本盤，美伊和談進度不確定性造成訂單復甦略有波折，但復甦方向沒有改變，預
估 2026 年營收下修 1.5%至 346.3 億元(原估 351.61 億元)，年增 0.6%，毛利率維持 25.2%之預估，
營益率略為上修 0.1ppt 至 14.6%(原估 14.5%)，營業利益下修 0.9%至 50.65 億元(原估 51.10 億元)，

年增 8.2%，稅後 EPS 上修 0.4%至 15.45 元(原估 15.39 元)，年增 5.3%。2026 年稅後 EPS 略為
上修係反映 1Q26 獲利優於預期。

### Page 5

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：成衣 2026 年 6 月 3 日 Makalot 第5 頁，共 17頁  
產業概況  
美國庫存偏低，回補庫存需求，有利 2026 年營運復甦 
近期美伊戰爭造成油價大漲，推升通膨預期，市場對於排擠消費的擔憂再起，而川普總統打壓油價
及抑制通膨，以推進降息的立場不變，且近期美伊和談進展，油價回落，舒緩通膨擔憂，後續須持
續關注美伊和談發展。我們對於 2026 年看法不悲觀，仍有一些兩個因素可以期待，其一為美國可
望啟動回補庫存，其二 2026 年世足賽可望誘發新的需求，提振 2026 年營運，以下個別分析這兩
因素。 
首先觀察美國服飾零售銷售表現，2026 年 4 月服飾零售銷售額 275.34 億美元，月減 1.5%，年增
5.8%，較前月年增 7.6%放緩，惟連 20 個月 YOY 正成長，銷售表現仍屬穩健。另一方面，美國服
飾庫存則相對平穩，2026 年 3 月服飾零售庫存略增至 584.58 億美元，月增 0.1%，年增 0.2%，庫
存動能低於 1H25 之 2-3%的年增表現。至於服飾存貨週轉天數增加 0.9 天至 63.7 天，仍低於 2025
年初 68-69 天，以及 2023 至 2024 年的 70 天水準，整體庫存偏低。搭配觀察美國紡織品進口
2025 年陷入衰退，而 2025 年年終消費旺季，服飾銷售維持熱絡的動能，將持續去化服飾庫存，預
期 2026 年回補庫存的機率大幅提升。 
圖 4：美國服飾零售銷售趨勢圖

資料來源：Bloomberg、元大投顧整理

### Page 6

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：成衣 2026 年 6 月 3 日 Makalot 第6 頁，共 17頁  
圖 5：美國服飾庫存及天數趨勢圖

資料來源：Bloomberg、元大投顧整理

世足賽商機擴大，聚焦美國再次舉辦且擴大參賽隊伍之效益 
另一方面在於 2026 年最重要的運動賽事焦點就是第 23 屆世界盃足球賽，此次世足賽兩大特點，
美國再次舉辦世足賽以及第三次擴大參賽隊伍，預期賽事熱度會大幅擴散。  
美國相隔 32 年再次舉辦，且是北美三國美加墨共同舉辦，盛況空前。1994 年美國第一次舉辦時，
現場觀賽人口總數由前一屆義大利舉辦的 251.6 萬人增加 42.6%至 358.8 萬人，創歷史新高，後來
各屆賽事都沒有突破這數字。全球電視收視人口總數由前一屆 10.3 億人倍增至 21 億人，年增
103.9%，FIFA 評估世足賽整體商機規模由 3.4 億美元增至 5 億美元，增加 47.1%，美國第一次舉
辦世足賽的效益相當顯著。 
圖 6：歷屆世足賽現場觀賽人口總數趨勢圖

資料來源：FIFA、元大投顧整理

### Page 7

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：成衣 2026 年 6 月 3 日 Makalot 第7 頁，共 17頁  
圖 7：歷屆世足賽電視收視人口總數趨勢圖

資料來源：FIFA、元大投顧整理

2026 年將是第三次擴大參賽隊伍。第一次是 1982 年由 16 支擴大至 24 支，比賽場次由 38 場增至
52 場，現場觀賽人口總數由 154.5 萬人增至 211 萬人，增加 36.6%，電視人口由 6 億人增至 7.5
億人，增加 25%，FIFA 評估的世足賽商機規模由 8 千萬美元增至 1.5 億美元，增加 87.5%。第二
次則是 1998 年由 24 支擴大至 32 支，比賽場次由 52 場增至 64 場，不過現場觀賽人口總數由
358.8 萬人降至 278.5 萬人，年減 22.4%，較不如預期，而電視人口則由 21 億人增至 28.8 億人，
年增 37.1%，FIFA 評估世足賽商機規模由 5 億美元增至 15.6 億美元，增加 212%。

展望 2026 年世足賽，將是第三次擴大參賽隊伍，由 32 支擴大至 48 支，比賽場次會由 64 場暴增
至 104 場，比賽期間也會拉長，世足賽風潮將更熱烈，保守預估現場觀賽人口來到 550 萬人，年
增 61.6%，樂觀版應有機會突破 650 萬人。電視人口則可來到 80 億人，年增 60%，FIFA 預估商
機規模擴大至 110 億美元，年增 45.3%。預期在世足賽觀賽人口增加，商機擴大的帶動，對於足球
鞋及運動鞋，與足球相關服飾的銷售均有提振作用，提振成衣及製鞋族群於 2026 年重啟成長軌道，
仍可正向期待。

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：成衣 2026 年 6 月 3 日 Makalot 第9 頁，共 17頁  
獲利調整與股票評價  
復甦略有波折，但復甦方向未變，2026 年重啟成長，評價偏低 
展望 2026 年，美伊和談進度不確定性恐影響 2H26 美國消費動能以及聚陽來自美系客戶的訂單展
望，不過品牌客戶庫存陸續去化，回補庫存的訂單需求仍在，只是尚待觀察戰爭發展，壓抑短期下
單意願，惟聚陽日系客戶訂單穩健，可成長雙位數，來自 Dick’s Sport 訂單亦可年增雙位數，
Under Armour 訂單有機會年增 50%，Gap 旗下運動服飾品牌 Athleta 訂單恢復增長，運動服飾訂
單需求維繫營運基本盤，美伊和談進度不確定性造成訂單復甦略有波折，但復甦方向沒有改變，預
估 2026 年營收下修 1.5%至 346.3 億元(原估 351.61 億元)，年增 0.6%，毛利率維持 25.2%之預估，
營益率略為上修 0.1ppt 至 14.6%(原估 14.5%)，營業利益下修 0.9%至 50.65 億元(原估 51.10 億元)，
年增 8.2%，稅後 EPS 上修 0.4%至 15.45 元(原估 15.39 元)，年增 5.3%。2026 年稅後 EPS 略為
上修係反映 1Q26 獲利優於預期。 
美伊和談的不確定性干擾聚陽來自美系客戶的訂單展望，惟考量 :1) 5 月營收 27.09 億元，月增
3.9%，年增 8.8%，動能穩健。2) 預估 2Q26 營收 81.77 億元，年增 6.3%，稅後 EPS 為 3.21 元，
年增 28.4%，維持年增格局。3) 預估 3Q26 營收 93.63 億元，年增 5.3%，稅後 EPS 為 4.09 元，
年增 17.6%，延續復甦。4) 戰爭造成訂單復甦略有波折，但復甦方向沒有改變，預估 2026 年營收
346.3 億元，年增 0.6%，稅後 EPS 為 15.45 元，年增 5.3%，重啟成長，目前本益比約 14.6 倍，
在 14 至 24 倍歷史本益比區間的下緣，評價偏低，投資價值浮現，重申買進建議，目標價維持 310
元(20 倍 PER X 2026 年稅後 EPS)。

公司簡介  
公司成立於 1990 年 1 月，是亞太地區知名的成衣製造商，主要業務為成衣的設計、製造及銷售，其客戶為全球及區域型之
大型成衣零售商，是全球第五大成衣代工商。 2015 年公司切入機能性服飾領域，運動服飾佔營收比重逐年提升。公司五大
廠區包括印尼、柬埔寨、越南、菲律賓、中國大陸，採「台灣接單，全球生產」的營運模式。公司成衣商品主要銷售地區
為美國市場，主要客戶包含百貨公司 (Kohl's JC Penney) 、大型賣場(Target、Wal-mart)、成衣專賣店 (Gap、Old Navy 、
Hanesbrands、Carter's、MANGO、Under Armour 等)；歐洲市場客戶有 ZARA、H&M 等，亞洲市場客戶包括台灣網購品
牌 Lativ、日商迅銷集團等。

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：成衣 2026 年 6 月 3 日 Makalot 第14 頁，共 17頁

ESG 分析  
分別藉由「在 ESG 議題上的曝險」和「個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力」兩個面向來衡量公司於 ESG 上的表現。 
► ESG 總分： 聚陽整體的 ESG 風險評級屬於低風險，於 Sustainalytics 資料庫所覆蓋的公司中排名亦排行於領先的位置，
在奢華服飾行業中的公司中排名領先同業。 
► 在 ESG 議題上的曝險： 聚陽的整體曝險屬於低等水準，略低於紡織品行業平均水準。紡織業中的公司通常較需要改善
的 ESG 議題包含人力資本、商業道德和人權供應鏈等。 
► 個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力：聚陽在針對重大 ESG 議題的管理水準和執行力為平均水準。公司採取了一些措施來
管理與重大 ESG 問題相關的風險，但這些措施不足以管理其所有風險。儘管如此，該公司尚未捲入任何具有重大財務
影響的風險事件。

投資評等說明 
買進：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持正面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中增持該股。 
持有-超越同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力高於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
持有-落後同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力低於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
賣出：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持負面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中減持該股。 
評估中：本中心之預估、評等、目標價尚在評估中，但仍積極追蹤該個股。 
限制評等：為遵循相關法令規章及/或元大之政策，暫不給予評等及目標價。

在原料成本增加推升客戶下單意願 ， 預估鑽全2Q26 營收金額 7.10
億元， YoY-4.21% ，QoQ+12.81% ，預估營業利益 0.28 億元，

YoY+35.2%

年，在車用電動工具呈現持平，建築用電動工具出貨成長帶動下，

收金額 3.20 億元， YoY+35.2% 。

四. 預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 1.30 元 
鑽全 2026 年在建築用電動工具持續推出新產品 ， 加上氣動工具客
戶持續回補庫存，預期鑽全 2026 年營收將恢復成長動能，惟電動
工具處理新品推廣期毛利率較低，拖累本業呈現衰退。宏遠預估
鑽全 2026 年營收金額 29.30 億元， YoY+1.17% ，預估營業利益
1.48 億元 ，YoY-15.33% ， 預估稅後盈餘1.80 億元 ，YoY-14.78% ，
預估稅後 EPS 1.30 元。

貳、公 司 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介 
鑽全成立於 1983 年，並於 1999 年掛牌上櫃、 2000 年轉上市，
資本額 13.86 億元，鑽全為全球最大氣動打釘槍 ODM 廠商，主要
產品包括氣動打釘機、瓦斯打釘機、車用氣動衝擊板手、磨光機
與研磨機。氣動打釘機主要銷售地區以美國為主，主要原因在於
美國房屋建築以木造房屋居多，內部陳設亦多木製品，且房屋市
場活絡、造價便宜、建築時間短、汰換率高，加上每年春夏災害
頻繁，房屋修繕情形多，為全球氣動打釘機最大消費國。鑽全以
ODM 為主，產品均在台灣設計、研發與組裝，對於產品的品質可
嚴格控制，因此國際打釘機品牌均為鑽全客戶。

圖一、鑽全歷史沿革

資料來源：鑽全、宏遠投顧整理

二. 2026 年營運概況及展望

鑽全生產基地皆位於台中工業區，共有八個生產基地，包括研發
中心、模具廠、壓鑄廠、塑膠射出廠、加工 &塗裝廠、加工中心、
熱處理廠、廠務中心 &組裝廠，提供客戶完整服務，並提供垂直整
合系統，具掌握具關鍵技術性零組件生產及品質控制。

鑽全整體營收地區比重，美洲地區占 41~42% 、歐洲地區占
30~31%、 亞洲地區與其他地區占25~30%。 工業用釘機旺季在2Q
與 3Q，氣動工具（汽修）旺季在 1Q 與 4Q，鑽全整體來說旺季
依序為 3Q、2Q、1Q 與 4Q。

預估 2026 年成槍營收金額 13.0 億元， YoY-8.1%

成槍，主要有氣動打釘機與瓦斯打釘機，主要用於傢俱製造、裝
橫、木造房屋、棧板與木箱的製造與建構。

鑽全成槍 2025 出貨量 45.13 萬枝， YoY-8.27% ，貢獻 2025 年營
收金額 14.14 億元， YoY-3.2% 。回顧 2025 年，受到美國對等關
稅影響，導致客戶訂單轉趨保守，拖累成槍營收衰退。

鑽全成槍 1Q26 營收金額 2.49 億元， YoY-46.06 。觀察 2026 年前
四月美國新屋開工數 425.6 萬棟， YoY+1.26% 。在大環境無太大
變化，鑽全將拓展北美地區以外市場，推出新產品，以抵銷北美
地區不利因素，宏遠預估 2026 年成槍出貨量 40 萬枝，
YoY-11.37% ，預估貢獻 2026 年成槍營收金額 13.0 億元，
YoY-8.1% 。

預估 2026 年電動工具營收金額 3.2 億元， YoY+35.2%

鑽全以氣動工具起家，近年來看好電動工具發展趨勢，也將其視
為近年營運重點項目，鑽全在電動化領域研發已投入 10 年，鑽全
2023 年已出貨歐洲客戶首批電動工具訂單，出貨量 1.1 萬枝，貢
獻 2023 年營收金額 1 億元。鑽全 2024 年電動工具出貨量 1.18
萬枝 ，YoY+7.27% ，貢 獻2024 年營收金額 0.83 億元 ，YoY-17.3% 。

1736 喬山(Johnson Health)

報告日期： 2026/06/02 研 究 員：洪  國 峰 (Titan Horng)  
壹、投 資 建 議

一. 公告 1Q26 稅後 EPS 0.08 元 
喬山公告 1Q26 營收金額 117.92 億元， YoY+11.15% ，
QoQ-39.13% ，營業利益 -0.93 億元， YoY-206.5% ，
QoQ-104.89% ， 稅後盈餘0.24 億元 ，YoY-91.34% ，QoQ-98.6% ，
稅後 EPS 0.08 元。

二. 預估 2Q26 稅後 EPS 1.98 元 
喬山 04/2026 營收金額 39.98 億元， YoY+6.4% ，MoM-6.31% 。
預估喬山 2Q26 營收金額 133.03 億元， YoY+8.1% ，
QoQ+12.81% ， 預估營業利益10.96 億元 ，YoY+31.29% ，較 1Q26
由虧轉盈，預估稅後盈餘 6.00 億元， YoY+436.25% ，
QoQ+2409% ，預估稅後 EPS 1.98 元。

三 . 預估 2026 年商用市場營收金額 14.3 億美元，
YoY+15.98%  
商用市場 1Q26 營收金額 2.35 億美元， YoY+17.21% 。商用市場
2026 年營運成長動能包括 ：1， 在歐美健身房主要客戶滲透率持續
提升，另外也拓展歐美各國本土前五大健身房客戶。 2，產品線擴
展，增加高階健身器材產品線，以便拓展高端健身房市場，增加新
客群。 3，商用健身器材零組件主要以台灣與中國上海為主，預期
在越南工廠生產比重提升帶動下，將有助整體毛利率提升。 預估喬
山 2026 年商用市場營收金額 14.3 億美元， YoY+15.98% 。

四. 預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 13.00 元 
喬山 2026 年在歐美健身房客戶持續在海外設據點 ， 加上客戶轉單
效益 ， 搭配旗下越南廠生產效率提升與產能擴充 ， 將有助喬山2026
年營運持續成長。宏遠預估喬山 2026 年營收金額 630.00 億元，
YoY+15.96% ，預估營業利益 59.86 億元， YoY+66.66% ，預估稅
後盈餘 39.44 億元， YoY+43.43% ，預估稅後 EPS 13.00 元。

五. 投資建議買進，目標價 156 元 
觀察喬山歷史 PER 落在 8~33X，2026 年 ROE 26.7%，PER 9.1X，
評價在低檔水準，加上 2026 年營運成長，故投資建議買進，目標
價 156 元。 (12X 2026 年稅後 EPS)

貳、公 司 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介  
喬山健康科技創立於 1975 年，資本額 30.34 億元，並於 2003 年
掛牌上市，喬山為亞洲第一、世界前三大的國際專業運動健身器
材集團公司，產品包括電動跑步機、室內健身車、橢圓訓練機、
重量訓練器材、划船器材、電動按摩椅、心肺訓練器材等。喬山
以「健康、價值、共享」為企業經營理念，專注於健康科技事業
的發展，並以 Matrix 、Vision 和 Horizon 自有品牌行銷全世界 80
餘國。生產基地位於台中、上海、與美國越南，其中台中生產高
單價的商用產品、上海主要生產製造量大的家用產品與小部分商
用產品、美國主要生產製造特殊重量訓練產品、越南主要生產製
造家用產品為主。

圖二、喬山全球銷售佈局

二.品牌定位與銷售通路  
喬山旗下擁有 Matrix 、Vision 和 Horizon 三項自有品牌，其中
Martrix 主打高價位商用市場 ， 其主要銷售對象包括健身房 、 飯店、
運動員訓練中心、大型公司、運動專賣店等； Vision 主打中高價
位的市場，主要銷售對象以大型公司、運動專賣店為主； Horizon
主打家用市場為主，銷售對象包括運動專賣店、電商與大型運動
器材專賣店。

三. 2026 年營運概況及展望

全球健身器材市場 100 億美元 (宏遠投顧推估 )，其中商用市場 50
億美元、家用市場 50 億美元。觀察全球主要廠商 2024 年營收金
額概況： Life 6.75 億美元， YoY+4.2%( 宏遠投顧推估 )；TG 7.67
億美元 ，YoY+13.6%；喬 山10.39 億美元 ，YoY+18.3%；Precor2.61
億美元， YoY+26.1% ；Pelonton 27.0 億美元， YoY-1.1% 。

預估 2026 年商用市場營收金額 14.3 億美元， YoY+15.98%

喬山商用市場客戶包括 ： 健身房北美星球健身 、 歐洲BASIC-FIT 、
拉丁美洲 Smart Fit 以及全球連鎖性健身房 Anytime Finess 、台 灣
World GYM 與健身工廠等；飯店客戶 Marriott 、STARWOOD 、
FOUR SEASONS 等。

圖五、喬山健身房客戶

資料來源：喬山 &宏遠投顧彙整

商用市場 2025 年營收金額 12.33 億美元， YoY+18.67% 。觀察喬
山商用市場 2025 年營運成長動能包括： 1，在中國大陸健身器材
出口至美國課徵高額關稅 ， 相對於台灣與越南關稅較低，帶動健身
房客戶訂單轉向台灣與越南地區。 2，歐洲大型客戶持續拓展美國
市場，另外美國主要客戶也拓展歐洲市場，歐洲與美國主要客戶
2025 年分別成長 25~28% 與 21~24% 。3，歐洲地區受惠中小型客
戶需求增溫，帶動歐洲地成長 25~30% 。

商用市場 1Q26 營收金額 2.35 億美元， YoY+17.21% 。商用市場
2026 年營運成長動能包括 ：1， 在歐美健身房主要客戶滲透率持續
提升，另外也拓展歐美各國本土前五大健身房客戶。 2，產品線擴
展，增加高階健身器材產品線，以便拓展高端健身房市場，增加新
客群。 3，商用健身器材零組件主要以台灣與中國上海為主，預期
在越南工廠生產比重提升帶動下，將有助整體毛利率提升。 預估喬
山 2026 年商用市場營收金額 14.3 億美元， YoY+15.98% 。

圖六、擴展商用市場新產品 /新通路

資料來源：喬山 &宏遠投顧彙整

預估 2026 年家用市場營收金額 4.00 億美元， YoY+12.68%

喬山家用市場主要以運動專賣店、電商與大型運動器材專賣店為
主。喬山為拓展家用市場零售通路，陸續在 2015 年~2016 年購併
2nd Wind 、BUSY BODY 、Leisure Fitness 與 Gym Source 等健
身器材銷售通路，喬山在北美銷售通路據點共 103 家，據點數占
北美 30% 。

家用市場 2025 年營收金額 3.55 億美元 ，YoY+17.16% 。喬 山2025
年家用營運成長主要包括： 1，BowFlex 在 2025 年貢獻營收金額
1.1~1.2 億美元， YoY+53~67% 。2，觀察歐洲地區與拉丁美洲受
惠家用健身器材營運基期較低，商用健身房興起帶動家用市場需
求熱絡，帶動歐洲地區與拉丁美洲家用市場分別成長 45~48% 與
22~25% 。3，觀察全球主要家用健身器材廠商，生產基地集中在
中國大陸，在美國對中國大陸出口產品課徵高額關稅因素，許多
訂單己移至越南生產，帶動喬山越南廠稼動率提升。

家用市場 1Q26 營收金額 1.01 億美元， YoY+12.85% 。家用市場
2026 年營運成長動能包括： 1，家用健身器材經歷 2020~2021 年
上半年疫情期間大好， 2021 下半年至 2023 年庫存調整， 2025 年
因關稅訂單偏保守，觀察零售通路庫存已達相對低檔水準，加上
己有廠商退出市場，將有助家用市場成長。 2，喬山持續拓展零售
據點效益顯現，例如大型量販店己向喬山採購多項家用產品線，
帶動大型量販通路營收。 3，家用旗下 BowFlex 在 2026 年推出新
產品，搶攻北美家用市場，將有助 BowFlex  2026 年出貨成長。
綜合上述，預估 2025 年喬山家用市場營收金額 4.00 億美元，
YoY+12.68% 。

預估富士醫療 (含自有品牌按摩椅 )貢獻 2026 年營收金額 1.70 億
美元， YoY+6.9%

喬山 10/29/2019 公告，以 67 億日幣 （約當台幣20 億元），向「朝
日生活與健康股份有限公司 Asahi Life & Health Corporation 」買
下子公司「富士醫療器股份有限公司 FUJI MEDICAL 
INSTRUMENT 」 60% 股權，「富士醫療器股份有限公司 FUJI 
MEDICAL INSTRUMENT 」為日本最大按摩椅製造商，擁有業界
領先產品開發和品牌實力，另外在日本佈有綿密的銷售通路，觀
察富士醫療器年營收金額 180~200 億日幣 (約當美元 1.8~2 億美
元)，己於 03/2020 完成股權交割。喬山 12/09/2022 公告取得富
士醫療器 40% 股權 ， 交易完成後喬山持有富士醫療器100% 股權。

富士醫療 2025 年營收金額 1.28 億美元， YoY+9.4% 。自有品牌按
摩椅 2025 年營收金額 0.31 億美元， YoY+6.9% 。觀察富士醫療營
收成長主因在於富士醫療拓展日本以外的外銷市場有成。

富士醫療 + 自有品牌按摩椅 1Q26 營收金額 0.36 億美元，
YoY+9.09% 預期 2026 年，在富士醫療持續拓展日本以外的外銷
市場，加上喬山自有品牌按摩椅穩定成長。預估富士醫療 2026 年
營收金額 1.36 億美元， YoY+6.25% ；預估喬山自有品牌按摩椅
2026 年營收金額 0.34 億元 ，YoY+9.68%， 合計兩者貢獻喬山2026
年營收金額 1.70 億美元， YoY+6.9% 。

叁、業 績 及 獲 利 預 估

預估 2026 年稅後盈餘 39.44 億元 ，YoY+43.43% ，稅 後EPS 13.00
元

喬山 2026 年在歐美健身房客戶持續在海外設據點 ， 加上客戶轉單
效益 ， 搭配旗下越南廠生產效率提升與產能擴充 ， 將有助喬山2026
年營運持續成長。宏遠預估喬山 2026 年營收金額 630.00 億元，
YoY+15.96% ，預估營業利益 59.86 億元， YoY+66.66% ，預估稅
後盈餘 39.44 億元， YoY+43.43% ，預估稅後 EPS 13.00 元。

公告配發現金股利 4.5 元，現金殖利率 3.8%

喬山歷年股利政策，主要以現金股利為主。回顧 2024 年，喬山股
利政策，配發現金股利 4 元，股利發放率 50% 。喬山公告 2025
年股利政策，將配發現金股利 4.5 元，現金殖利率 3.8% 。

股價相對大盤走勢

南亞科 (2408 TT)  Trading Buy

系統性風險，調降至 Trading Buy 。  
投資建議 ： Kioxia(258A JP) 、 SanDisk(SNDK US) 、 Solidigm 及
Cisco(CSCO US) 認購南亞科私募普通股，對南亞科而言，此為
具策略意義的合作，除挹注資金外，亦有助於綁定長期客戶需
求，並提升拓展未來 CSP 等大客戶的機會。供給端方面， DRAM
大廠持續將資本支出與產能轉向 HBM 與先進製程 DDR5，壓縮
傳統 DDR4 與 LPDDR4 供給；需求端受惠 AI 伺服器與邊緣運
算帶動記憶體搭載量提升，支撐整體 DRAM 報價維持高檔。在
價格上行帶動下，預估南亞科 2026 年稅後 EPS 達 54.47 元。過
去 3 個月記憶體產業面臨多項利空雜音，包括現貨價格波動、
中國供給擴張疑慮、終端需求放緩，及近期市場對 NVIDIA Vera
平台 SOCAMM2 配置調整的擔憂。然而， DRAM 及 NAND 產品
報價持續上漲，顯示產業供需結構及基本面未出現明顯轉弱跡
象。考量短期市場波動加劇，因此將投資評等調降至 Trading 
Buy ，待市場情緒及籌碼結構趨穩後再重新檢視投資機會。  
1Q26 稅後 EPS 7.55 元：1Q26 營收為 490.87 億元 ，QoQ+63.11% ，
南亞科 DRAM 平均銷售價格較 4Q25 增加 ，ASP 約 QoQ+>70% 。
匯率對營收影響 QoQ+1~+3% ，Shipment QoQ-4~-6% 。毛利率受
Blend ASP 上升與匯率有利影響 ， 1Q26 毛利率從 4Q25 的 49.04%
上揚至 67.87% ，增加 18.83 個百分點。 1Q26 稅後 EPS 7.55 元，
淨值 62.25 元。  
2026 年資本支出提升至 520 億元 ：南亞科董事會通過 2026 年
的資本支出預算以不超過新台幣 520 億元為上限， YoY+287% ，
其中 62 億元是 2025 年遞延至 2026 年，資本支出中 70% 用在新
廠的廠務無塵室等，剩下 30% 則會使用在設備採購上。預估新
廠將在 2027 年初裝機， 1H27 正式量產，規劃是 1H28 可以達到
月產能 15~20K 片 /月的目標，以 DDR5 為主。目前 DDR5 占南
亞科約 10% ，公司目標是維持在 10% 以上並逐步提升，但仍需
視整體缺貨結構而定。

### Page 2

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 6 月 8 日

2 群益投顧 
  南亞科 ESG ：  
佈局綠色科技生產  
DRAM 為智慧世代的關鍵零組件，南亞科研發先進且具高效能的 「環境友善
產品」以符合市場與客戶需求。所有產品於設計初期時，即導入生命週期思
維與綠色設計，透過綠色產品推動委員會進行綠色產品管理。

重視人才發展與培養  
「人才」是公司創新、營運與永續發展的根本，強化公司全方位人才培育與
發展，南亞科技與長庚大學簽署成立記憶體學程碩士班，學生除可獲高額獎
學金，更可獲得畢業後的就業機會。南亞科技期盼從育才、用才、引才、聚
才、留才各方面，強化臺灣記憶體產業在國際上的競爭力。

共同攜手永續夥伴  
南亞科攜手上下游合作夥伴一同前行， 295 家供應商認同公司的永續理念，
一同簽署南亞科技企業社會責任承諾書且簽署率達 100% 、輔導供應商進行
永續改善，稽核、輔導與改善率均 100% 完成，並持續辦理供應鏈研討會。
此外，也與供應商共同合作推動人權及環境相關的議題。

永續績效備受肯定  
南亞科視 ESG 為企業的重要承諾，南亞科追求經營績效的同時，也同步落
實 ESG 的平衡發展。南亞科技參加 2025 第 18 屆台灣企業永續獎評選，公
司已連續第九年獲頒多項殊榮，獲獎包括年度最佳報告 (製造業 )、台灣 100
大永續典範企業獎及四項永續單項績效  – 氣候領袖獎、創新成長領袖獎、
資訊安全領袖獎與透明誠信領袖獎。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌，亦將出具降評報告。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

創見 (2451 TT)  Trading Buy

後續毛利率擴張有限 ， 下調至Trading Buy ，目 標 價393 元。 
投資建議 ： AI 需求佳，大幅提升記憶體需求，目前原廠供應的
合約滿足率低於 50% ，創見雖然也與原廠簽訂長期供貨合約
（ LTA ） ，但原廠仍是依照既定配額供貨，創見目前庫存水位約
100 多億元， 2Q26 庫存金額還會增加，但主要是單價上升，實
際數量變化不大。目前 DRAM 與 Flash 供給都相當吃緊，雖 3
大原廠已投入大量資本支出擴產，但最快也要到 2027 年才陸續
開出新產能，推升報價進一步上揚，預估創見 2026 年稅後 EPS 
106.10 元。創見公告 05/2026 合併營收達 62.98 億元， MoM-
15.46% 、YoY+469.83% ；累計 04/2026~05/2026 營收達 137.5 億
元，高於 1Q26 整季營收 136.3 億元。記憶體報價仍處上升趨
勢，支撐獲利成長，惟隨市場預期價格漲勢趨緩，毛利率擴張
空間有限，因此下調至 Trading Buy ，目標價 393 元。  
1Q26 稅後 EPS 18.86 元：因 AI 需求爆發引發結構性缺貨，記
憶體供不應求，在工控市場剛性需求帶動下，創見 1Q26 營收為
136.28 億元， QoQ+100.31% ，毛利率  76.39% ，季增 14.77 個百
分點，稅後純益  81.22 億元， QoQ+148.95% ，稅後 EPS 18.86
元。毛利率高於同業主因在於自動化與生產效能優勢，及低成
本結構，包括許多廠房已使用 20、 30 年，折舊接近完成，全球
據點也多為自有廠房，因此整體營運成本低於同業。  
創見工業用佔比 70% 以上： 創見 2026 年初工控相關應用營收
佔比已升至  70% 以上， 2Q26 占營收比重增加至 90% ，主要是
價格高，消費性通路銷售明顯下滑。創見工控以高階嵌入式市
場為主，涵蓋工業用 SSD 、 Flash 、記憶卡與  DRAM 模組，廣
泛應用於 AIoT 、醫療、交通及強固型電腦。創見在記憶體上游
原廠宣布產品 EOL 之前，已提前 2 年鎖定採購量與長約配額，
讓公司在市場面臨 DRAM 和 NAND Flash 舊世代缺料潮時，能
確保穩定的出貨能力。因工控產品需長期穩定供貨，且為客製
化需求，毛利率較高，是創見撐住缺貨潮、達成高獲利的主力。

2 群益投顧 
  創見 ESG ：  
創見官網將  ESG 分別對應環境 （永續環境） 、社會 （社會公益） 與治理 （公
司治理） ，並於 2025 年成立永續發展委員會來推動，涵蓋政策規劃、成效
檢視與跨部門執行。  近期成效包括修訂 《永續發展實務守則》 、納入  TCFD 
氣候風險評估，以及持續發行年度永續報告書。  
E：環境  
創見強調廢棄物管理、節能措施、有害物質審查與低碳轉型。官網寫到公司
投入環保產品開發，並響應氣候變遷規畫減碳政策，透過評估風險與機會來
降低能源消耗與碳排放。  
S：社會  
社會面聚焦維護社會公益與利害關係人權益。官網依《永續發展實務守則》
承諾善盡責任，透過永續發展委員會的社會公益小組推動相關活動。  
G：治理  
治理面由行政副總經理任永續負責人，設立公司治理小組處理政策與資訊
揭露。官網強調董事會監督、跨部門協作，並將  ESG 納入管理方針。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌，亦將出具降評報告。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

以 不 用 派 人 到 府 解 決 ， 此 晶 片 會 整 合 進 Router /Gateway 裡 面 ， 目 前 此 產 品

產業速報

5 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (StrongBuy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (TradingBuy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 ， 亦 將 出 具 降 評 報 告 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

股現金股利 4 元 ，盈餘配發率達 190%，超 額 配 發。以06/10/2026

### Page 2

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 6 月 11 日

2 群益投顧 
  興富發建設 ESG： 
興富發遵循全球永續性標準理事會 GSSB 發布之 GRI Standards 、聯合國永
續目標 SDGs 、同業標竿案例、營造及不動產行業補充指南蒐集永續議題，
結合歸納出包含公司治理、環境及社會公益等 3 大面向中 26 項永續議題。
公司治理方面， 11/14/2023 董事會通過設立永續發展委員會，並於 2024 年
度召開 3 次例行性會議；環境永續方面，以蒐集相關氣候風險資料、分析評
估、制度相應策略、定期檢視執行成效及瞄準未來方面等 5 大步驟執行風險
評估，並推出台中七期市政萬象廣場、台中市政壹號廣場等微型商辦。以智
慧建築為建築平台 ， 打造兼具建築裝置 、 自動化及通訊網路系統 ， 並成立BIM
建築資訊模型中心，降低耗能。社會公益方面，持續著墨於在地關懷及地方
創生，落實多元共榮、公益志工假。  
公司簡介： 
興富發建設成立於 01/23/1980 ，前身為鋐福建設股份有限公司，主要經營業
務包含國民住宅出租出售及委託營造業者興建商業大樓。 05/1989 因合併宏
巨建設，更名為宏巨建設股份有限公司， 2000 年再次合併興富發建設股份
有限公司，於 05/06/2003 正式更名為興富發建設。目前主要推案區域涵蓋
全台各地，包括大台北、桃園、新竹、台中、台南、高雄等都市中心。期下
建設事業有興富發建設、潤隆建設、博元建設及齊裕建設；飯店事業有 3 個
據點、 3 家營運管理合作夥伴及新北北海溫泉洲際酒店、台南安平亞樂軒飯
店、高雄凱悅酒店。  
在手開發案： 
興富發建設在手建案共 38 筆，預估合計總銷 4,957.52 億元，主要分布於中
南部。台北 3 筆，總銷 444.74 億元；新北 2 筆，總銷 607.66 億元；桃園 4
筆，總銷 390.01 億元；新竹 1 筆，總銷 147.89 億元；台中 14 筆，總銷 1,810.3
億元；台南 2 筆，總銷 149.41 億元；高雄 12 筆，總銷 1,407.51 億元。未來
仍將積極參與合建案開發，都更與危老都市更新，且以商辦住宅雙軌並行，
主要鎖定重大建設發展、優質學區及公園綠地、交通便利與機能成熟區域等
議題。

30%；換屋族出售舊屋的期限延長半年至 18 個月，且 09/20/2024( 含 )後之貸
款案件皆適用； 2026 年起銀行不動產放款總量將回歸由各銀行內部自行控
管，不再需要給出預定目標，僅須按月向央行彙報相關資料即可；自
03/20/2026 起全國自然人第 2 戶購屋貸款成數上限由 50% 上修至 60% 。  
公司表示，在 09/2025 新青安不計入銀行法 72-2 後，首購族群有感受到善
意， 10/2025 整體銷售率略增。期望政府可針對都更危老案件開放更多融資
限制。另外，公司亦表示微型商辦、商辦市場 (企業總部 )因需求、總價及區
域面等因素，將為未來發展趨勢。  
2026 年大手筆配發現金股利 4 元： 
2026 年將配發 2025 年度每股現金股利 4 元，盈餘配發率達 190% ，超額配
發，顯示公司對其未來各年度完工案量深具信心。以 06/10/2026 收盤價換
算，現金殖利率約 8.8% 。公司表示，未來股利政策仍將維持年配機制及高
盈餘配發率，並以現金股利為主。

將於 2~3Q/26 完工，預期多數 3Q26 初啟動交屋。 3Q26 底 ~4Q26 初亦有望
開始國家企業廣場、當代文匯、蘿曼羅蘭、森悅等認列入帳。考量到 2026
年將有 12 筆新案完工交屋，加上先前已完成 2025 年部分低毛利案去化，以
及潤隆 4Q25 有 3 筆遞延交屋及溫莎堡、潤隆鉑悅等 2 筆新案交屋，有望貢
獻 320 億 元 營 收。預 期2026 年營收 、 獲利倍數成長 。 研究部預估興富發2026
年營收 1,002.38 億元 ，YoY+222.76% ，稅 後 純 益140.59 億元 ，YoY+216.85% ，
稅後 EPS 6.47 元 。 若以過去約80~100% 現金配發率中上緣為基礎 ， 推估 2027
年有望配發 6 元現金股利，以 06/10/2026 收盤價換算，現金殖利率達 13%。
不畏房市冷淡，2026 年預計推案千億，展現多年經營韌性： 
2026 年預計推出 7 筆新案，合計總銷 1,155.91 億元。包括新北國家壹號廣

9 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌，亦將出具降評報告。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

Goldman Sachs (Asia) L.L.C., Taipei
Branch
Lotes (3533.TW): Taiwan Computex & Corporate Day: Server remains the
key growth driver , despite ongoing material cost & supply risk
3 June 2026 | 6:52PM CST
We hosted meetings with Lotes at our Taiwan Computex & Corporate Day (June
2nd). Key takeaways include:
(1) Management expects CPU-related product shipments to grow ~20% YoY in 2026,
after factoring in potential material/component supply shortage, while key

shortages include CPU, memory, PCB, and passive components.
(2) Lotes continues to make progress in new AI-related products and expects to
begin shipping SoCAMM in 3Q26 (Lotes is the first supplier for Vera Rubin), and also
expects NPO products to start contributing in 2027. Management expects AI-related
products to account for 2-3% of total revenue in 2026, with contribution expanding
to double-digit over the next 3-5 years.
(3) Lotes expect raw material cost pressure on GM to persist into 2Q26. However,

management expects GM to improve in 2H26, driven by the release of new AMD
Venice CPU, which will see ASP increase of at least 15% vs. previous generation (23%
increase in pin count vs. AMD’s SP5 socket).
(4) The company continues to engage in price negotiations with clients, while pricing
for AMD Venice has already incorporated higher material costs. Management
mentioned the base case is no price down for the existing and new products (vs.
typical industry practice with price erosion of ~5% in year 1, ~3% in year 2 and ~1%

in year 3 after new product release).
(5) For the CPU platform, Lotes mentioned Eagle Stream penetration increased from
75-80% in 1Q26 to current 80-85%, while Birch Stream should further increase to
20-30% by end of 2026 from the current 15%. For AMD, Venice should account for
15-20% (revise down from 20-25%) of total servers by the end of 2026 but with
majority of contribution from high-end solution (SP7 with 7536 pins) and expects to
see contribution from the low-end solution (SP8 with 5572 pins) starting in 2027.

Investment view
We maintain our relative cautious view on Lotes’ business outlook, considering (1)
the rising material cost environment might sustain and the company has adopted a
stable pricing rather than cost pass through via price increase, given the risk of losing
market share and (2) limited contribution from new AI-related products (including
SoCAMM, QD, GPU socket and CPO/NPO socket). We continue to see strong CPU
server demand in near term and extend to coming years (see AMD report here

www.gs.com/research/hedge.html

which we believe will be a key growth driver for Lotes in near-to-mid term; however, we

Lotes is a connector supplier mainly focusing on the niche CPU socket market with more
than 40% share in 2024, but we see pressure for its market share outlook in the coming
years considering the intensifying competition in the server CPU market. We maintain

our positive view on the company’s GM expansion potential longer term, which we
believe will be mainly driven by better product mix (Venice server CPU to be released in
2H26 and Oak Stream server CPU to be released in 2027, both with better ASP and
margin). Meanwhile, Lotes’ CPU socket product line’s ASP/GM are higher than the
company average, and we see scope for both to grow even faster in the new product
generation given the larger socket size and pin count required. However, the company’s

new product line, Quick Disconnects (UQD or MQD) for liquid cooling systems, is likely to
deliver very limited revenue/gross profit c
ontribution in the coming years (0.4/1.0%
revenue and 0.2/0.7% GP contribution in 2026E/2027E) given thermal solution
providers typically ship cold plates as integrated modules and leading thermal suppliers
tend to secure QD supply in-house or through affilia
ted suppliers. We are Neutral rated
on Lotes considering the share loss risk in server sockets and the shares trading at the

historical peak P/E level, making valuation less attractive, despite the company’s solid
revenue/GM expansion trend driven by socket upgrading demand. Key risks: (1)
stronger-/weaker-than-expected server shipment growth which would raise/lower
server CPU socket demand; (2) lower-/higher-than-expected component costs without
product pricing adjustment; (3) slower-/faster-than-expected AI server-related
contribution
Valuation methodology: Our 12-month target price of NT$1,790 is based on a target

2H26E-1H27E EPS.

(2) lower-/higher-than-expected component costs, and (3)
slower-/faster-than-expected AI server-related contribution

GS Factor Profile
The Goldman Sachs Factor Profile provides investment context for a stock by comparing key attributes to the market (i.e. our universe of rated stocks)
and its sector peers. The four key attributes depicted are: Growth, Financial Returns, Multiple (e.g. valuation) and Integrated (a c omposite of Growth,
Financial Returns and Multiple). Growth, Financial Returns and Multiple are calculated by using normalized ranks for specific metrics f or each stock. The

normalized ranks for the metrics are then averaged and converted into percentiles for the relevant attribute. The precise calcula tion of each metric may
vary depending on the fiscal y ear, industry and region, but the standard approach is as follows:
Growth is based on a stock’s forward-looking sales growth, EBITDA growth and EPS growth (for financial s tocks, only EPS and sales growth), with a
higher percentile indicating a higher growth company. Financial Returns is based on a stock’s forward-looking ROE, ROCE and CROCI (for financial

stocks, only ROE), with a higher percentile indicating a company with higher financial r eturns. Multiple is based on a stock’s forward-looking P/E, P/B,
price/dividend (P/D), EV/EBITDA, EV/FCF and EV/Debt Adjusted Cash Flow (DACF) (for financial s tocks, only P/E, P/B and P/D), with a higher percentile
indicating a stock trading at a higher multiple. The Integrated percentile is calculated as the average of the Growth percentile, Financial Returns

percentile and (100% - Multiple percentile).
Financial Returns and Multiple use the Goldman Sachs analyst forecasts at the fiscal y
ear-end at least three quarters in the future. Growth uses inputs
for the fiscal y ear at least seven quarters in the future compared with the year at least three quarters in the future (on a per-share basis for all metrics).
For a more detailed description of how we calculate the GS Factor Profile, please c ontact your GS representative.

M&A Rank
Across our global coverage, we examine stocks using an M&A framework, considering both qualitative factors and quantitative factors (which may vary
across sectors and regions) to incorporate the potential that certain companies could be acquired. We then assign a M&A rank as a means of sc oring
companies under our rated coverage from 1 to 3, with 1 representing high (30%-50%) probability of the company becoming an acquisition targ et, 2
representing medium (15%-30%) probability and 3 representing low (0%-15%) probability. For companies ranked 1 or 2, in line with our s tandard

departmental guidelines we incorporate an M&A component into our target price. M&A rank of 3 is considered immaterial and theref ore does not
factor into our price target, and may or may not be discussed in research.
Quantum
Quantum is Goldman Sachs’ proprietary database providing access to detailed financial statement histories, forecasts and ratios. It can be used for
in-depth analysis of a single company, or to make comparisons between companies in differ ent sectors and markets.

by Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research and referred to in this research.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a B uy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not C overed), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region ’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the r eturn across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment R eview Committee or

other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such s tocks.
Total return potential represents the upside or downside differ ential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all cov ered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership .

Greater China Technology Semiconductors

Industry View Attractive
Old Memory: Readacross from
Kioxia IR Day
Legacy NAND technology powers high performance SSD: On its IR Day (link),

Kioxia (covered by Kazuo Yoshikawa) highlighted its GP series high-performance
SSD, which features 100m+ IOPS, ultra-fast and low-latency performance to
accelerate next-gen AI. The company said it is compatible with Nvidia’s Storage-Next
solution. The GP series SSD is used to expand GPU memory to resolve system
memory constraints, and enables large-scale DB
(database) and faster generation.
The GP series SSD (XL-Flash) has adopted Kioxia's proprietary SLC chip.

Implications for Greater China memory stocks: We mentioned in our note, Old
Memory: Upside Surprise Ahead, that legacy NAND technology could be ideal for
fast reading and writing speeds and hence could see expanded TAM from adoption
in datacenters. We view the confirmation from Kioxia as positive for GigaDevice,
Winbond, and Macronix, which are legacy NAND vendors in our coverage and remain
OW on all three stocks.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Macronix International Co Ltd (2337.TW)
Base case, P/B, in line with our Greater China memory coverage, due to the memory industry's
cyclical nature. We apply a target multiple of 7.0x to our 2026 BVPS estimate, vs. its historical
average of 1.5x since 2017. We continue to believe the P/B target is justifiable, as The MLC and
legacy TLC NAND could be in greater shortage into 2H26, with under supply easily going up

to 40%. The only supplier which could fill the gap remains Macronix.
Risks to Upside
n Wider legacy NAND supply / demand gap
n NOR up-cycle, driven by limited supply expansion and/or stronger demand
n Faster growth for Switch hardware and core titles
Risks to Downside
n Less severe legacy NAND supply / demand gap
n NOR down-cycle, driven by faster supply expansion and/or demand weakness
n Slower growth for Switch hardware and core titles
Winbond Electronics Corp (2344.TW)

Base case, 5.5x 2026e P/B. This method is in line with the approach we adopt for Greater China
memory IDM peers, in view of the industry's high volatility. We believe the stock merits a mul-
tiple beyond the high end of its historical band of 0.5-1.5x since 2017, as we are seeing DRAM
pricing upside, a sustainable NOR price hike, and opportunities in SLC NAND and SiCap
foundry business.
Risks to Upside
n NOR Flash pricing increase, driven by stronger-than-expected demand

n Stronger-than-expected DRAM pricing given ongoing supply shortage
n Faster-than-expected SLC NAND development
Risks to Downside
n NOR Flash downcycle, driven by weaker demand
n Lower-than-expected DRAM pricing given ongoing oversupply
n Slower-than-expected SLC NAND development
GigaDevice Semiconductor Beijing Inc (603986.SS)
Our price target is our base case value, derived from our residual income model. Key assump-
tions: cost of equity of 8.9% (beta 1.1, risk-free rate 2% and risk premium 6%), a payout ratio

of 40%, medium-term growth rate of 16.4%, and a terminal growth rate of 3.0%.
Risks to Upside
n NOR up-cycle driven by stronger demand
n Superior chip design, leading to increasing exposure to low-density NOR
n Faster-than-expected DRAM development

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

Goldman Sachs (Asia) L.L.C.
Pegatron (4938.TW): Computex 2026: AI server in expansion; AI
infrastructure product portfolio showcased
4 June 2026 | 9:13PM HKT
We visited Pegatron’s booth on June 2 at our Computex tour in Taipei. Pegatron
showcased its AI infrastructure across cloud and edge AI, as well as quadruped
robot. Management remains positive on their AI server business growth and expects
continuous growth in coming years.
Key takeaways: Pegatron showcased its complete next-generation AI infrastructure

product portfolio at Computex (link), such as Nvidia Vera Rubin NVL72, Nvidia HGX
Rubin NVL8, and AMD GPU servers, focusing on the deployment of AI Factory,
High-Performance Computing (HPC), and AI data center. Pegatron’s 400G, 800G, and
1.6T switch were also showcased to fulfill the high-speed and lo w latency data
transmission requirements of AI data center. Management sees the growth
opportunities in AI infrastructure and expects a continuous expansion for its AI

server business in coming years, supported by the company’s transformation and
expansion from consumer electronics to AI servers. Besides AI infrastructures,
Pegatron also launched its next generation SIMBA II quadruped robot platform,
capturing the growth opportunities in edge AI computing and supporting the
company’s revenue growth ahead.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Pegatron
We are Sell rated on Pegatron with a 12-month target price of NT$66.2. Our TP is based
on a 10.0x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E multiple
correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s share performance.
Key risks: 1) stronger-than-expected smartphone market recovery, 2)
faster-than-expected AI server ramp-up, and 3) stronger-than-expected demand on

consumer electronics.

GS Factor Profile
The Goldman Sachs Factor Profile provides investment context for a stock by comparing key attributes to the market (i.e. our universe of rated stocks)
and its sector peers. The four key attributes depicted are: Growth, Financial Returns, Multiple (e.g. valuation) and Integrated (a c omposite of Growth,
Financial Returns and Multiple). Growth, Financial Returns and Multiple are calculated by using normalized ranks for specific metrics f or each stock. The

normalized ranks for the metrics are then averaged and converted into percentiles for the relevant attribute. The precise calcula tion of each metric may
vary depending on the fiscal y ear, industry and region, but the standard approach is as follows:
Growth is based on a stock’s forward-looking sales growth, EBITDA growth and EPS growth (for financial s tocks, only EPS and sales growth), with a
higher percentile indicating a higher growth company. Financial Returns is based on a stock’s forward-looking ROE, ROCE and CROCI (for financial

stocks, only ROE), with a higher percentile indicating a company with higher financial r eturns. Multiple is based on a stock’s forward-looking P/E, P/B,
price/dividend (P/D), EV/EBITDA, EV/FCF and EV/Debt Adjusted Cash Flow (DACF) (for financial s tocks, only P/E, P/B and P/D), with a higher percentile
indicating a stock trading at a higher multiple. The Integrated percentile is calculated as the average of the Growth percentile, Financial Returns

percentile and (100% - Multiple percentile).
Financial Returns and Multiple use the Goldman Sachs analyst forecasts at the fiscal y ear-end at least three quarters in the future. Growth uses inputs
for the fiscal y ear at least seven quarters in the future compared with the year at least three quarters in the future (on a per-share basis for all metrics).
For a more detailed description of how we calculate the GS Factor Profile, please c ontact your GS representative.

M&A Rank
Across our global coverage, we examine stocks using an M&A framework, considering both qualitative factors and quantitative factors (which may vary
across sectors and regions) to incorporate the potential that certain companies could be acquired. We then assign a M&A rank as a means of sc oring
companies under our rated coverage from 1 to 3, with 1 representing high (30%-50%) probability of the company becoming an acquisition targ et, 2
representing medium (15%-30%) probability and 3 representing low (0%-15%) probability. For companies ranked 1 or 2, in line with our s tandard

departmental guidelines we incorporate an M&A component into our target price. M&A rank of 3 is considered immaterial and theref ore does not
factor into our price target, and may or may not be discussed in research.
Quantum
Quantum is Goldman Sachs’ proprietary database providing access to detailed financial statement histories, forecasts and ratios. It can be used for
in-depth analysis of a single company, or to make comparisons between companies in differ ent sectors and markets.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a B uy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not C overed), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region ’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the r eturn across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment R eview Committee or

other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such s tocks.
Total return potential represents the upside or downside differ ential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all cov ered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership .

Asustek Computer Inc.  | Asia Pacific
Strong Preliminary April Profits
Asustek reported April 2026 profit numbers per TWSE regulatory requirements.
• April 2026 revenue was NT$84.9bn.
• April 2026 NP was NT$5,313mn (+283% y/y), which accounts for 42% and
50% of our and the Street's 2Q26 NP estimates, respectively.
•  NpM was 6.3%
Backdrop: On its 1Q26 earnings call in May (report), Asustek reported a 1Q results
beat and provided strong 2Q26 guidance for its PC (+10-15% q/q) and server

businesses (+50% q/q).
This aligns with Lenovo's comment that PC pull-ins will persist into 2Q (
report),
though Asustek's OpM guidance appeared to be overly conservative at only 4-5%,
vs, April NpM of 6.3%..
Exhibit 1: Asustek's preliminary April 2026 profit numbers

Key Takeaways
Asustek's April profits imply upside risks to both our 2Q estimates and the
Street's.
However, we hold to our view that the market will repay the pulled-forward
demand sometime in 2H...
...when PC prices rise more meaningfully for consumers, which will eventually
lead to demand destruction.
We also think this is priced in with the stock up 51%  over the past month (the

TAIEX is up 12% over the same period).
Within our Greater China PC OEM coverage, we have a relative preference for
Lenovo (EW) over Asustek (UW) and Acer (UW).

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Lenovo (0992.HK)
Base case, residual income model. Our key assumptions include a cost of equity of 9.3% (beta
of 1.1, risk-free rate of 2.0%), a medium-term growth rate of 5.0%, and a terminal growth rate
of 3.0%.
Risks to Upside
n Faster-than-expected smartphone and data center turnaround
n Stronger PC demand recovery in China
n Better cost savings and improved product mix
n Lower-than-expected increase in memory price

Risks to Downside
n Slower-than-expected smartphone and data center turnaround
n Weaker PC demand recovery in China
n Deterioration in cost savings and product mix
n Higher-than-expected increase in memory price
Acer Inc. (2353.TW)
Base case, residual income model. We use RI model for Acer's valuation. Key assumptions
include a cost of equity of 9.2% (beta of 0.9, equity risk premium of 10.2% and risk-free rate
of 0.5%), a 6.0% medium-term growth rate, and a 3% terminal growth rate.

Risks to Upside
n Stronger macro recovery than expected
n Share gains in the PC market
n Better-than-expected cost controls
n Lower-than-expected increase in memory cost
Risks to Downside
n Weaker macro recovery than expected
n Share loss in the PC market
n Worse-than-expected cost controls
n Higher-than-expected increase in memory cost
Asustek Computer Inc. (2357.TW)
Base case, sum of the parts:
We apply a 9x multiple to Asustek's 2026 brand profits. This is somewhat below the midpoint

of its historical band of 7-15x since 2009, which we view as fair because memory prices are
rising and will have a negative impact on margins.
We value its stakes in Pegatron and Advantech based on our price targets in proportion to
Asustek's holdings.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

We reiterate our Buy rating on Delta, and raise our 12-m TP from
NT$2,420 to NT$4,500 (implying 96% upside) to reflec t better

visibility on the long-term growth opportunity for AI power
products (we expect Delta’s overall AI power revenue, including
AC/DC PSU+ DC/DC converter + Power rack ex. PSU, to go up by
210% 2025-28E CAGR), whose profitability should r
emain at a high
level in coming years (we believe 50-60%+ for component level
products), and the company’s stable industry leadership position,
based on our BOM cost analysis (Exhibit 4
).
Our key finding in this r eport is that we see the AI PSU BOM cost per

watt going up with higher power density (43% higher BOM per watt
from 5.5kW PSU to 12kW PSU), which basically suggests a higher
ASP per watt for Delta in coming years (we expect overall AI PSU
industry ASP per watt will grow at a 40% 2025-28E CAGR), as the
semiconductor and passive components will all continue to be
upgraded. Moreover, given the adoption of new AI Power Rack will
start from late 2026 (with a
ccelerating demand YTD), we expect a
new spec of 18.3kW PSU demand to ramp up quickly, which, based

on our understanding, has a similar BOM / watt as the 5.5kW
product; however, the high wattage requirement still suggests a high
ASP/GM for Delta.
Moreover, the adoption of power rack (+-400V / 800V) from late
2026 will not only benefit Delta with 100% r
edundancy on PSU (vs.
current power shelf solution redundancy ratio at 40-60%), it should
also lead to a better DC/DC content for Delta, which in module level
Delta has a dominant share. We now expect the AI DC/DC converter

market should see a 142% 2025-28E CAGR, while contributing to
Delta’s 2026/27/28E revenue by 5%/9%/14% with 40%+ OPM, or
12/16/20% OPI contribution in 2026/27/28,
Overall, we revise up Delta’s 2026/27/28E earnings estimate by
1/5/24/% to better reflec t the solid demand uptrend from the

Earnings revision and where we stand vs. consensus
Earnings revisions
We revise up our 2026/27/28E EPS by 1/5/24% to factor in higher ASP per watt for its AI
PSU business and higher penetration for its power rack business (we raise up revenue
estimates by 2/23% for 2027/28E). For the HVDC solution, we expect demand in late
2026 with faster progress from the +-400VDC solutions, and continue to expect
stronger HVDC demand in 2027E/2028E. Therefore, we revise up our 2026/27/28E GM

by 0.1/0.5/0.1ppt due to the slight margin expansion driven by better scale.
Where are we vs. consensus?
Our 2026/27/28E revenue estimates are 7/20/47% higher than Bloomberg consensus,
due to our positive view on the AI power related business opportunity (we believe AI
power revenue, including AC/DC, DC/DC and power rack products, will reach
NT$156bn/NT$486bn/NT$1,434bn in 2026/27/28E). As such, considering our high
estimate for Delta’s AI power product GM at 50%, our 2026/27/28E GM estimate is

3.3/6.1/6.0ppt higher than Bloomberg consensus, as we believe the company’s revenue
will be mainly driven by AI power products in the coming years (20%/41%/66% revenue
contribution in 2026/27/28E).
As shown in Exhibit 17, our GM estimates for Delta in 2027/28 are much higher than
Bloomberg consensus, due to our more positive view on the GM expansion driven by AI
server/datacenter power related products, which should continue to see increasing
contribution to revenue in the coming years. We believe the market has been too

conservative on estimating Delta’s GM potential, given the fact that Delta has proven
that its AI product revenue/GM is much higher than company average (GM improved

### Page 11

Valuation and risks - Raise TP to NT$4,500
We reiterate our Buy rating on Delta with a new TP of NT$4,500 (from NT$2,420),
implying 96% upside. We believe the company will continue to deliver a solid earnings
growth rate (100%+ 2025-28E EPS CAGR) which is much faster than the company’s
historical average level (~10% CAGR in the past 20 years) and AI power industry peers
(15% CAGR).
Our 12-month target price is based on an unchanged 26.5x P/E, and we roll over our

valuation base from 2Q27-1Q28E to 2028E, discounted back to 2027 at a CoE of 11%,
given the much improved visibility on HVDC power rack adoption timeline. The 26.5x P/E
multiple is +1.6x STDC higher than Delta’s past 15-year average valuation, and we
believe Delta, as the key AI power product supplier with a solid market position (50%+
market share in coming years on GSe) should at least trade above historical valuation.
Our TP-implied 22.0x 2027-28E P/B is much higher than the company’s average P/B

multiple in the past (Exhibit 21); however, after considering the high ROE (increasing to
39%/58%/87% in 2026/27/28E based on GSe), we believe this higher implied multiple is
justified as the c ompany should be traded at a premium considering its accelerating
expansion in ROE in coming years.

Investment Thesis & PT methodology and risks
Delta Electronics is the largest vendor of power supply components globally (in terms of
sales) and a key servo motor/inverter/PLC supplier in mainland China. It is also a key
supplier for the EV/server power industry, holding one of the largest market shares in
both markets. We are positive on Delta’s long-term growth opportunity driven by
multiple factors (EV/EV charger/5G infrastructure/Datacenter power/ESS etc.) in the

coming years. We also expect demand for its AI server power and cooling systems to
deliver solid growth given the increasing demand for high computing performance AI
servers, which will require higher efficienc
y power components and better cooling
systems to improve the overall performance. Trading at a 1-year forward P/E multiple
that is below our target multiple, we believe the stock is undervalued, given the
company’s strong revenue outlook (50%+ 2025-28E CAGR) for its growth sectors

(including EV & EV charging / green capex & renewable energy / cloud computing /
smart manufacturing / satellite communication & smart connectivity), and its leadership
in the power component industry. We are Buy rated. Key downside risks include: (1)
slower-than-expected AI server power consumption growth momentum, (2) potential
market share loss risk in AI server DC-DC power system or the potential design change
on AI server DC-DC system, and (3) slower than expected of future products

deployment, including power rack, SST and SOFC.
Valuation methodology: We are Buy rated on Delta. Our 12m TP of NT$4,500 is based
on a target P/E multiple of 26.5x (1.6x s.d. higher than Delta’s past 15-year average
valuation) applied to our 2028E EPS and discounted back to 2027 at a CoE of 11%.
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected AI server power consumption growth
momentum, (2) potential market share loss risk in AI server DC-DC power system or the

potential design change on AI server DC-DC system, and (3) slower than expected of
future products deployment, including power rack, SST and SOFC.

deliver a solid ROE outlook in coming years

GS Factor Profile
The Goldman Sachs Factor Profile provides investment context for a stock by comparing key attributes to the market (i.e. our universe of rated stocks)
and its sector peers. The four key attributes depicted are: Growth, Financial Returns, Multiple (e.g. valuation) and Integrated (a c omposite of Growth,

Financial Returns and Multiple). Growth, Financial Returns and Multiple are calculated by using normalized ranks for specific metrics f or each stock. The
normalized ranks for the metrics are then averaged and converted into percentiles for the relevant attribute. The precise calcula tion of each metric may
vary depending on the fiscal y ear, industry and region, but the standard approach is as follows:
Growth is based on a stock’s forward-looking sales growth, EBITDA growth and EPS growth (for financial s tocks, only EPS and sales growth), with a

higher percentile indicating a higher growth company. Financial Returns is based on a stock’s forward-looking ROE, ROCE and CROCI (for financial
stocks, only ROE), with a higher percentile indicating a company with higher financial r eturns. Multiple is based on a stock’s forward-looking P/E, P/B,
price/dividend (P/D), EV/EBITDA, EV/FCF and EV/Debt Adjusted Cash Flow (DACF) (for financial s tocks, only P/E, P/B and P/D), with a higher percentile
indicating a stock trading at a higher multiple. The Integrated percentile is calculated as the average of the Growth percentile, Financial Returns

percentile and (100% - Multiple percentile).
Financial Returns and Multiple use the Goldman Sachs analyst forecasts at the fiscal y
ear-end at least three quarters in the future. Growth uses inputs
for the fiscal y ear at least seven quarters in the future compared with the year at least three quarters in the future (on a per-share basis for all metrics).
For a more detailed description of how we calculate the GS Factor Profile, please c ontact your GS representative.

M&A Rank
Across our global coverage, we examine stocks using an M&A framework, considering both qualitative factors and quantitative factors (which may vary
across sectors and regions) to incorporate the potential that certain companies could be acquired. We then assign a M&A rank as a means of sc oring
companies under our rated coverage from 1 to 3, with 1 representing high (30%-50%) probability of the company becoming an acquisition targ et, 2
representing medium (15%-30%) probability and 3 representing low (0%-15%) probability. For companies ranked 1 or 2, in line with our s tandard

departmental guidelines we incorporate an M&A component into our target price. M&A rank of 3 is considered immaterial and theref ore does not
factor into our price target, and may or may not be discussed in research.
Quantum
Quantum is Goldman Sachs’ proprietary database providing access to detailed financial statement histories, forecasts and ratios. It can be used for
in-depth analysis of a single company, or to make comparisons between companies in differ ent sectors and markets.

Source:                         Trendforce, Morgan Stanley Research
We expect TV panel prices to start trending down from 3Q26
and IT panels from 4Q26. Moreover, emerging businesses will
take time to deliver meaningful contribution. Maintain EW on TV

panel names, with a relative preference for BOE.
TV panel prices to stay flat MoM in June: We expect mainstream 32", 43", 55", 65"
and 75" TV panel pricing to stay flat MoM for the month of June. On the demand
side, as earlier restocking since the start of the year tapers off, panel order
momentum is slowing. On the supply side, panel makers continue to demonstrate
good discipline in controlling output. We continue to believe that TV panel
shipments could be below seasonal levels, given the strong 1H26, so panel makers’

bargaining power is likely to weaken further in the coming months. Thus, our base-
case assumption remains that TV panel prices should trend down from 3Q26, but
the decline should not be significant, as supply-side discipline should provide
downside support. On a quarterly average basis, we expect TV panel prices to
increase by low single-digit % QoQ in 2Q26.
IT panel prices to stay largely stable in June: Monitor panel prices edged up 0.2%
MoM, while NBs are expected to remain flat MoM. In general, we continue to expect

IT panel prices to remain relatively stable vs. TV panels in the coming quarters.
Opportunities for glass in advanced packaging will take more time: We have been
believers in the adoption of glass in advanced packaging architectures  (
see our deep
dive report) as its larger dimensions could offer better cost efficiency. Moreover, it
can provide better electrical performance with lower signal loss, reduced mismatch
in coefficients of thermal expansion among different materials to mitigate warpage,

and greater mechanical strength to resist distortion during manufacturing and
operation. That said, the supply chain is still optimizing processes, so this remains
less of a story for 2026–27.
Risk/reward less attractive: With panel prices likely to trend down from 3Q26, we
view valuations as less attractive at this stage, especially for Taiwan names AUO
(2409.TW; 1.4x P/B) and Innolux (3481.TW; 1.9x P/B). We acknowledge potential
upside from emerging businesses, where glass could be used in advanced packaging,

but meaningful contribution will take time. In addition, panel makers are not the
only players seeking to penetrate this market. We therefore view the investment
case for these names as less compelling on this angle, given valuations already
exceed peak levels of the past few years. Within China, we prefer BOE (000725.SZ;
1.7x P/B) over TCL (000100.SZ; 1.7x P/B) due to its scale advantage and higher
concentration in the display business.

and trading securities held by a research analyst account.

TV Panel Prices To See Stabilizing in June
TV panel prices started to edge up at the beginning of this year, rising 1% MoM in January,
2% MoM in February and 2% MoM in March.  April and May were largely flat,  and we see
prices stabilizing into June before order momentum potentially tapers from late 2Q26.

Panel Shipments To Be Skewed Toward 1H26

Over the past decade, TV panel shipments have averaged a 49%/51% split between 1H/2H.
In 2026, we expect the mix to skew toward 1H26, as TV brands have restocked ahead of
major promotional events, including the Winter Olympics in February, the FIFA World Cup
in June–July, and the China 618 campaign. While we see no clear evidence that these
events will significantly boost global TV demand, shipment timing has consistently shifted
between quarters around such promotions.

Display Fab Utilization to Stay at 80-85% in 2Q26
Supported by stronger TV panel shipments, display fab utilization averaged ~80% in 4Q24
and 82% in 1Q25. It declined to 78% in 2Q25 as brands turned more conservative after
earlier pull-ins tapered and the impact of China’s trade-in program faded. Utilization rose
to 80–85% in 3Q25 as restocking resumed for year-end promotions and remained at a
similar level in 4Q25 despite seasonal softness, as well as into 1Q26. We think industry

utilization can remain roughly flat QoQ at 80–85% in 2Q26, as output discipline remains
in place.
In the medium to long term, we maintain our view that the display segment is undergoing
structural change. Chinese panel makers have shifted strategically from top-line growth to
profitability, with fewer government subsidies reinforcing supply discipline. We think more
investors now believe the industry’s “production to order” model can remain sustainable,

especially as panel price cycles shortened to quarters in 2023–24 vs. multi-year cycles in
the prior decade. Consolidation moves – including LG Display’s sale of its Gen 8.5
Guangzhou fab to TCL and Sharp’s shutdown of its Gen 10 Sakai fab – also support a
healthier supply backdrop (for more information, see Display – Structural Changes on
Supply Side Not Fairly Priced In,  June 17, 2021).

Morgan Stanley Research 5
Risk/Reward Remains Less Attractive
We believe this round of panel price hikes is coming to an end, and thus risk/reward is less
attractive, especially for Taiwanese names. Below are our current views:
• AUO (2409.TW, Derrick Yang): We are EW on AUO,  with a PT of NT$14.00, based
on a  0.7x 2026e P/B multiple. Although TV panel price hikes are ongoing, we think
the valuation of 1.4x 2026e P/B has  priced in all favorable dynamics vs. its mid-

cycle average of 0.8x since 2022.
• BOE (000725.SZ, Derrick Yang): We have a relative preference for BOE, owing to
its scale advantage, more diversified revenue mix across applications and
technologies, and relatively cheap valuation vs. its Chinese peer TCL. Our PT of
Rmb5.20 is based on 1.4x 2026e P/B vs. its mid-cycle average of 1.2x since 2022,
and we think the premium is justified by its solid industry position.
• Innolux (3481.TW, Derrick Yang): We are EW on Innolux, with a price target of NT

$19.50, based on a  target multiple of 0.7x 2026e P/B (vs. mid-cycle average of 0.5x
since 2022). We think the valuation  premium implied by our target multiple over
its mid-cycle average  can be justified by the consolidation of Pioneer from
December 2025, which will likely bring its commodity revenue down to 45-50% in
2026e vs. 66% in 2024. For the FOPLP business, we think it could leverage its
existing technology expertise and legacy capacity, but more meaningful

contributions are likely to take time.
• LG Display (034220.KS, Shawn Kim): Meaningful OLED revenue contribution
should increasingly improve earnings from 2H25, but potentially weaker end-
markets keep us from turning bullish on LGD. The stock trades at 1.1x 2026e P/B
vs. a historical mid-cycle level of 0.5x.
• TCL (000100.SZ, Derrick Yang): We are EW with a price target of Rmb4.70 (1.7x
2026e P/B).  The stock is trading at 1.7x 2026e P/B vs. its mid-cycle average of 1.4x

since 2022, so we think the panel price hike cycle has been mostly baked into its
share price.
• Corning (GLW.N, Meta Marshall): GLW's display business operates efficiently and
sees further margin support  from additional currency-related price increases, while
the optical business resumes growth as carrier fiber deployments pick up. GLW's
display business closely correlates with panel maker utilization, and is its largest
earnings contributor.
• Greater China DDIC supply chain implications – Novatek (3034.TW, Daniel Yen):

Daniel's UW thesis is based on  weaker PC, smartphone and auto demand, gross
margin pressure and lack of new growth drivers. That said, Novatek's OLED DDI
roadmap looks more competitive than that of its key Korean competitors on
performance and cost, helping  it increase its market share in Apple's iPhone supply
chain.

Morgan Stanley Research 7
Where Could We Be Wrong?
• Stronger end-demand: If end-demand for major applications turns out to be
better than expected, there could be  stronger panel shipments and less panel price
pressure.
• More stringent  output control: If major panel makers collectively adjust
utilization to an extreme extent, they could mitigate pricing pressure in the case of
softer demand.
• Non-commodity display business: If the non-commodity business progresses

faster than expected, panel makers could show better financial results.

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

## Document 114: 260607_ms_advantech

### Page 1

M
Update
Advantech  | Asia Pacific
May Sales -6% MoM/+32% YoY
In this report, we focus on Advantech's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price.
Details:
• May sales were NT$7,734mn (-6% MoM/+32% YoY).
• QTD revenue reached 75% of consensus at NT$21,274mn (+4% QoQ/+19%
YoY) and 77% of the company's guidance at NT$20,790mn (+2% QoQ/+17%
YoY at mid-point).
Our view:
• We see 2Q26 revenue tracking ahead of market expectations.

• Management sees limited GM impact from elevated component costs, as it
remains confident in passing most incremental costs to customers  (
see our
report).
• A solid B/B ratio continues to point to a strong revenue outlook, supported
by demand from the semi equipment, robotics, and smart manufacturing
segments.
• We remain constructive on Advantech, as it is well positioned to benefit
from accelerating IoT market growth amid edge AI proliferation.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
Valuation Methodology and Risks
Advantech (2395.TW)
Base case, 30x 2025e P/E. We view our target multiple as justified by our 19% earnings CAGR
forecast for 2024-26, as it implies a PEG of 1.6, vs. ~3 over 2014-21. It has also been tempered
too reflect tariff uncertainty.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected macroeconomic conditions
n More top- and bottom-line growth from the platform and applications
Risks to Downside

n Weaker macro environment
n Slower progress in WISE-PaaS and solution-ready packages

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Hiwin Technologies Corp.  | Asia Pacific
May Sales Flat MoM/+28% YoY
In this report, we focus on Hiwin's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price.
Details:
• May  sales were NT$2,480mn (flat MoM/+28% YoY).
• QTD revenue reached 66% of our 2Q26 estimate at NT$7,490mn (+17%
QoQ/+26% YoY) and 68% of consensus at NT$7,307mn (+15% QoQ/+23%
YoY).
Our view:
• We see 2Q26 revenue tracking in line with expectations.

• Hiwin should continue to see good revenue momentum in the coming
quarters amid the industry recovery trend, and might witness more
meaningful margin expansion in 2H26 on the back of price hikes and high
utilization.
• We stay constructive on the name, with its current valuation at 32x 2027e P/
E vs. peak cycle level of 35-40x.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Hiwin Technologies Corp. (2049.TW)
Base case, P/E. We think this valuation methodology better captures Hiwin's earnings growth
momentum, as its valuation tends to move with the business cycle. We apply a target multiple
of 37x to our 2027 EPS estimate. We view this multiple as justified by the 44% operating profit
CAGR we project for 2025-28.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger industrial automation demand pickup.

n Market share gains.
n Earlier-than-expected revenue contribution from humanoids.
Risks to Downside
n Weaker industrial automation demand.
n More pricing pressure.
n Market share loss.
n More broad-based impact on the global economy from geopolitical risks.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

### Page 1

M
Update
E Ink Holdings Inc.  | Asia Pacific
May  Sales +13% MoM/+1% YoY
In this report, we focus on E Ink's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price.
Details:
• May sales were NT$3,280mn (+13% MoM/+1% YoY).
• QTD sales reached 61% of our 2Q26 revenue estimate of NT$10,043mn
(+16% QoQ/-5% YoY) and 60% of consensus at NT$10,229mn (+18% QoQ/-
4% YoY).
Our view:
• We see 2Q26 revenue tracking slightly behind market expectations.

• That said, the April preliminary EPS of NT$1.02 points to some upside risk to
consensus estimates of NT$2.85 for 2Q26 (
see our report).
• Management maintained the guidance to grow by 20-25% in 2026, driven by
20-30% growth from ESLs, single digit % growth from e-readers/e-notes and
high single digit % of revenue contribution from signage (mostly retail media
and indoor applications).
• In the near term, we believe that the market is awaiting more data points to

support management's positive guidance post the recent rally from trough
level valuations.
• Longer term, we believe it will continue to benefit from e-paper demand
from various applications, given its dominant industry position.
• Stay OW with PT of NT$250 (19x 2027 P/E vs. currently at 15x).

Morgan Stanley Research 3
Valuation Methodology and Risks
E Ink Holdings Inc. (8069.TWO)
Base case, 19x 2027e P/E. We believe this valuation methodology best captures E Ink's earn-
ings growth momentum. We view our target multiple of 19x as fair, considering our estimated
20% earnings CAGR for 2025-28. The implied PEG of 1.0x is slightly higher than its typical level
in 2020-25, when the stock was trading at an average P/E of 21x one-year forward consensus

EPS with a 24% earnings CAGR.
Risks to Upside
n Firmer-than-expected ESL orders
n Stronger demand for e-readers and e-notes
n Better macro conditions
n Milder competition
n Brisker inventory digestion
Risks to Downside
n Softer-than-expected ESL orders
n Slower demand for e-readers and e-notes
n Macro headwinds
n Intensified competition
n More protracted inventory digestion

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Better than feared PC and new

Solid 2Q26 guidance: Elan guided for 2Q revenue of NT$3.2-3.4bn, with the  mid-
point implying a 2% Q/Q increase and 9% Y/Y growth.  2Q gross margin mid-point
guidance of 48.0% implies 0.4ppt Q/Q expansion as AI business ramps up. 2Q
operating margin mid-point guidance is 23.7%, slightly lower than 24.1% in 1Q26,
mainly due to more expense on photo mask and R&D.

AI drones and optics to add growth
: Besides strong  shipments of haptics
touchpads and 4-in-1 copilot key, Elan is expanding its business in the AI drone
market, and shipment started in June, targeting non-China market,  As for optics, Elan
cooperated with PETA Optronics and will provide EIC, including TIA and drivers.
Management expects a sample to be ready in 1H27 and products for shipment in
2H27 for scale out NPO modules.
Stay OW
given better-than-expected results, increasing haptic adoption and

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Elan Microelectronics Corp (2458.TW)
Residual income. We assume a cost of equity of 9.6%, an intermediate growth rate of
7.0%, payout ratio of 80% and terminal growth rate of 4.5%.
Risks to Upside
n Notebook shipments stronger than expected.
n AI PCs help content growth in notebook peripheral ICs.
n e-book controller IC ramps up faster then expected.
Risks to Downside
n Notebook replacement cycle extents, shipments lower than expected.

n No spec upgrade in notebook peripheral ICs in AI PC era.
n No progress in e-book controller IC.
